    27	  replicas: 1
    28	  selector:
    29	    matchLabels:
    30	      app: postgres-client
    31	  template:
    32	    metadata:
    33	      labels:
    34	        app: postgres-client
    35	    spec:
    36	      containers:
    37	        - name: postgres-client
    38	          image: postgres:16-alpine
    39	          command: ["sh", "-c", "sleep infinity"]
    40	          envFrom:
    41	            - secretRef:
    42	                name: remote-postgres

    23	            - weight: 100
    24	              preference:

    26	                  - key: kubernetes.io/hostname
    27	                    operator: In
    28	                    values:
    29	                      - k3s-worker1
    30

    32	        - key: "node.kubernetes.io/unreachable"
    33	          operator: "Exists"
    34	          effect: "NoExecute"
    35	
    36	        - key: "node.kubernetes.io/not-ready"
    37	          operator: "Exists"
    38	          effect: "NoExecute"

    42	          image: nginx:alpine

FILE: ./workloads/openmrs37/base/gateway.yaml
==========================================
     1	apiVersion: v1
     2	kind: ConfigMap
     3	metadata:
     4	  name: openmrs-gateway-nginx
     5	  namespace: openmrs37
     6	data:
     7	  default.conf: |
     8	    server {
     9	      listen 80;
    10	      listen [::]:80;
    11	
    12	      absolute_redirect off;
    13	
    14	      proxy_set_header Host $host;
    15	      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    16	      proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
    17	      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    18	      proxy_http_version 1.1;
    19	
    20	      location = /healthz {
    21	        access_log off;
    22	        default_type text/plain;
    23	        return 200 "ok\n";
    24	      }
    25	
    26	      location = / {
    27	        return 301 /openmrs/spa/home;
    28	      }
    29	
    30	      location = /openmrs/spa {
    31	        return 301 /openmrs/spa/home;
    32	      }
    33	
    34	      location = /openmrs/spa/ {
    35	        return 301 /openmrs/spa/home;
    36	      }
    37	
    38	      location /openmrs/spa/ {
    39	        rewrite ^/openmrs/spa/(.*)$ /$1 break;
    40	
    41	        proxy_pass
    42	          http://openmrs-frontend.openmrs37.svc.cluster.local:80;
    43	      }
    44	
    45	      location /openmrs {
    46	        proxy_read_timeout 300s;
    47	
    48	        proxy_pass
    49	          http://openmrs-backend.openmrs37.svc.cluster.local:8080;
    50	      }
    51	    }
    52	
    53	---
    54	apiVersion: apps/v1
    55	kind: Deployment
    56	metadata:
    57	  name: openmrs-gateway
    58	  namespace: openmrs37
    59	spec:
    60	  replicas: 1
    61	
    62	  selector:
    63	    matchLabels:
    64	      app: openmrs-gateway
    65	
    66	  template:
    67	    metadata:
    68	      labels:
    69	        app: openmrs-gateway
    70	
    71	    spec:
    72	      nodeSelector:
    73	        kubernetes.io/hostname: k3s-worker1
    74	
    75	      containers:
    76	        - name: gateway
    77	
    78	          image: openmrs/openmrs-reference-application-3-gateway:3.7.1
    79	          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
    80	
    81	          command:
    82	            - nginx
    83	            - -g
    84	            - daemon off;
    85	
    86	          ports:
    87	            - name: http
    88	              containerPort: 80
    89	
    90	          resources:
    91	            requests:
    92	              cpu: 100m
    93	              memory: 96Mi
    94	
    95	            limits:
    96	              cpu: 300m
    97	              memory: 256Mi
    98	
    99	          volumeMounts:
   100	            - name: nginx-config
   101	              mountPath: /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
   102	              subPath: default.conf
   103	              readOnly: true
   104	
   105	          readinessProbe:
   106	            httpGet:
   107	              path: /healthz
   108	              port: 80
   109	
   110	            initialDelaySeconds: 3
   111	            periodSeconds: 5
   112	            timeoutSeconds: 2
   113	            failureThreshold: 6
   114	
   115	          livenessProbe:
   116	            httpGet:
   117	              path: /healthz
   118	              port: 80
   119	
   120	            initialDelaySeconds: 10
   121	            periodSeconds: 10
   122	            timeoutSeconds: 2
   123	            failureThreshold: 3
   124	
   125	      volumes:
   126	        - name: nginx-config
   127	
   128	          configMap:
   129	            name: openmrs-gateway-nginx
   130	
   131	---
   132	apiVersion: v1
   133	kind: Service
   134	metadata:
   135	  name: openmrs-gateway
   136	  namespace: openmrs37
   137	spec:
   138	  type: NodePort
   139	
   140	  selector:
   141	    app: openmrs-gateway
   142	
   143	  ports:
   144	    - name: http
   145	      port: 80
   146	      targetPort: 80
   147	      nodePort: 30366

    68	                  -h 127.0.0.1
    69	                  -uroot
    70	                  -p"${MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD}"

    72	            periodSeconds: 5
    73	            timeoutSeconds: 3
    74	            failureThreshold: 12

     1	apiVersion: v1
     2	kind: ConfigMap
     3	metadata:
     4	  name: openmrs-gateway-nginx
     5	  namespace: openmrs
     6	data:
     7	  default.conf: |
     8	    server {
     9	      listen 80;
    10	      listen [::]:80;
    11	
    12	      absolute_redirect off;
    13	
    14	      proxy_set_header Host $host;
    15	      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    16	      proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
    17	      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    18	      proxy_http_version 1.1;
    19	
    20	      location = /healthz {
    21	        access_log off;
    22	        add_header Content-Type text/plain;
    23	        return 200 "ok\n";
    24	      }
    25	
    26	      location = / {
    27	        return 301 /openmrs/spa/home;
    28	      }
    29	
    30	      location = /openmrs/spa {
    31	        return 301 /openmrs/spa/home;
    32	      }
    33	
    34	      location = /openmrs/spa/ {
    35	        return 301 /openmrs/spa/home;
    36	      }
    37	
    38	      location /openmrs/spa/ {
    39	        rewrite ^/openmrs/spa/(.*) /$1 break;
    40	
    41	        proxy_pass http://openmrs-frontend.openmrs.svc.cluster.local:80;
    42	      }
    43	
    44	      location /openmrs {
    45	        proxy_read_timeout 300s;
    46	
    47	        proxy_pass http://openmrs-backend.openmrs.svc.cluster.local:8080;
    48	      }
    49	    }
    50	
    51	---

    74	        - name: gateway

    76	          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
    77	
    78	          ports:
    79	            - name: http

    81

    84	              cpu: 100m
    85	              memory: 96Mi
    86	            limits:
    87	              cpu: 300m
    88	              memory: 256Mi
    89	
    90	          volumeMounts:
    91	            - name: nginx-config
    92	              mountPath: /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
    93	              subPath: default.conf
    94	
    95	          readinessProbe:
    96	            httpGet:
    97	              path: /healthz
    98	              port: 80
    99	            initialDelaySeconds: 3
   100	            periodSeconds: 5
   101	            timeoutSeconds: 2

   103

   105	            httpGet:
